# Mosaic Features

Plain-English description of every feature, where to find the code, and test status.
Update the status column after running `npx vitest run`.

---

## Marketing-ready feature list

Short, user-facing descriptions for App Store copy, landing pages, social. Consultant tone, no superlatives. Engineering catalogue starts below the next divider.

### One app, every endurance goal
- Marathon, triathlon, cycling, and heavy-load sports in a single plan engine.
- On-Demand mode for athletes who don't want a fixed plan: generate a session whenever you want one. (The premium "let Coach build a context-aware workout" path is hidden for App Store v1; the free rule-based generator stays.)
- Mode switching preserves history. Train for a marathon this year, a 70.3 next year, without resetting.

### Plans that adapt to what you actually did
- Every week is regenerated from the sessions you completed, skipped, or pushed.
- Skipped sessions get pushed forward once, then drop with the prediction adjusted honestly.
- Holiday, illness, and injury modes reshape the plan around real life, not around the plan.
- Replace-and-reduce: swap a hard session for an easier one in two taps and the rest of the week rebalances.
- Every workout card shows its total time, and it updates live as you adjust. Distance runs show distance and time; structured sessions show time. (All modes.)
- Every workout carries its pacing (2026-06-04): easy and long runs show their easy-pace target ("7km @ 6:10/km") alongside the paces threshold/VO2/MP sessions always had; swim speed reps show an absolute fast pace when CSS is known; rolling bike endurance shows its Z2 watt range when a power meter is present. Run targets regenerate each render so they track marker updates automatically. (Sprint/max-effort items are intentionally target-less.) Pace factors live in one canonical map (`RUN_PACE_FACTOR_VS_EASY`, `src/workouts/intent_to_workout.ts`). ✅ tested, ❌ not device-verified.
- Adjust any session on the fly: a per-session slider scales the whole session up or down (easy runs, tempos, and intervals all shrink proportionally to the time you have). There's also an Easier/Harder intensity control: running steps the pace ladder (easy→marathon→threshold→VO2), bike steps a %FTP ladder (endurance→tempo→sweet-spot→threshold→VO2, showing the live watt target), and swim steps a CSS-pace ladder (showing the live pace target). One-tap "Got less time" presets too. The change applies to that session only. Your plan, targets, and race prediction stay put, and a deliberately shorter session is scored as done, not missed. **Available in all modes**: running, triathlon, cycling, and HYROX (run + bike/erg/swim sessions). HYROX stations/bricks have no intensity control (round/rep-structured). The adjustment **persists across reloads** — running scales re-attach to their session by a stable slot id (so a plan reshuffle can't lose them), and triathlon/cycling/HYROX scales are written straight onto the stored session object. HYROX stations/bricks and gym sessions (rep/round-structured) are deferred to a follow-up. **Targets follow the adjustment** (2026-06-04): stepping intensity rewrites the prescription numbers in the description — run paces retarget to the new ladder type from the live easy pace (`retargetDescriptionPace`), bike watt ranges scale by the ladder %FTP ratio (`scaleWattRanges`), swim paces become CSS + the new rung's offset (`setSwimPaceTokens`) — so the card, the detail modal's Target line, and the adherence grader all agree. Adjusted sessions saved before this build heal at render (`modDisplayDescription` at every mod re-apply site, canonical: `src/ui/week-resolve.ts`). (Code: `src/ui/workout-adjust-overlay.ts`, `src/workouts/workout-scaling.ts`; tri/HYROX entry points in their workout detail modals. ✅ helper-tested; running device-verified by Tristan 2026-06-01; retarget ❌ not device-verified.)

### Plans that start where you actually are
- You can start any day of the week, not just Monday. Onboard on a Wednesday and week 1 begins Wednesday — the earlier days aren't shown as blank cards or counted as missed, and anything you already trained this calendar week still counts toward your load. Week 1 is naturally lighter (fewer days), and full volume resumes the next Monday. Onboard on a Saturday or Sunday and week 1 is just a chill 1–2 session lead-in, with your first full week landing the following Monday. (Code: `dropPreOnboardSessions` in `src/ui/workout-status.ts`, applied in the running/tri/HYROX week resolvers; adherence pro-rata in `plan-adherence.ts`; auto-skip filter in `events.ts`. ✅ unit-tested; ❌ not device-verified.)
- Week 1 volume is anchored to your current weekly km — no cold-start jump from 18 km/wk to 50 km/wk.
- No synced history? A weekly km you type at onboarding sets a running plan's starting volume, how fast it builds, and its weekly load target. The first sync replaces it with your real history. (Code: `planRampArchetype` in `src/calculations/athlete-archetype.ts`, `typedVolumeWeeklyTSS` in `src/calculations/fitness-model.ts`; SCIENCE_LOG §DF. ✅ unit-tested; ❌ not device-verified.)
- At low weekly volume the plan gives you a few longer runs rather than a scatter of pointless 15-min jogs. Easy runs never drop below 25 minutes; when the week's volume can't fill every run-day at that length, run-days are dropped and the survivors lengthened, climbing back to your chosen frequency as volume ramps. A short note on the Plan view explains the lower run-count in early weeks. (Code: `applyHoursBudget` in `src/workouts/plan_engine.ts`; `buildRunDayRampNote` in `src/ui/plan-view.ts`. ✅ tested; not device-verified.)
- If onboarding finds no running data at all (no synced history, nothing typed), the review step makes you set a rough weekly volume on a slider before "Build my plan" — the volume budget needs a starting number to ramp from, otherwise week 1 can land too hard. The slider shows no default ("Drag to set") so it doesn't bias your answer; a rough estimate is enough. (Code: `renderVolumeRow` mandatory branch + `hasNoRunningVolumeSignal` in `src/ui/wizard/steps/review.ts`. ✅ tsc/lint/suite; ❌ not browser/device-verified.)
- The plan recognises seven athlete types: returning runner, consistent runner, cyclist learning to run, court-sport crossover, sedentary novice, and others. Each gets its own ramp slope.
- Bone and tendon adapt slower than cardio, so non-impact crossovers (cyclists, swimmers) ramp at 5%/week. Detrained runners with marathon history ramp at 10%/week to reflect muscle memory.
- The forecast reflects the actual ramp. A plan that takes 12 weeks to reach peak gets a smaller predicted improvement than one that's already there — honest about what you can deliver.
- Athletes heavier than the general-population average can opt into a size-tuned plan: some easy running is gently swapped for low-impact cross-training (bike, swim, elliptical) to keep cumulative joint load manageable without losing aerobic fitness. Opt-in at onboarding, off any time, framed as physics rather than a verdict on body type. From there the app adapts to you live from your HR and pace.

### Honest race predictions
- Marathon, triathlon, and HYROX finish times update weekly from your training.
- Triathlon predictions account for course profile, water type, expected temperature, and altitude.
- Heat penalty scales with body size. Larger athletes store heat faster, so their hot-weather forecast and hot-day training analysis account for the added strain (needs weight and height).
- HYROX predictions place you against 849,443 real finishers from seasons 8 and 9, by format and sex with per-station splits, and use your own station test results. Untested stations start from the median split of your ability band, and the doubles and Pro conversions come from the same results.
- HYROX race plan: per-run and per-station split targets and a race clock for your goal, next to the forecast. Where you lost time compares your last race, segment by segment, with athletes who finished in the same time.
- Cross-training counts where the science says it should. If you're fit from other aerobic sport (cycling, rowing, swimming, team sport), the app trusts your fitness more for shorter races instead of assuming low running mileage has cost you, and your race-day forecast climbs faster because an existing aerobic engine converts to running quicker than building one from scratch. The marathon stays judged on long-run endurance, which cross-training does not replace. Mark it with the "Also fit from other sports" toggle at onboarding (auto-detected from your logged activity); the prediction page tells you when it moved your number. (Code: `crossTrainingPredictionCredit` in `predictions.ts`, `CROSS_TRAINED_FORECAST_PREMIUM` in `training-horizon.ts`; SCIENCE_LOG §BV/§BX. ✅ unit-tested, ❌ not device-verified.)
- Race-day outcomes feed back into the model so future predictions get sharper.
- Old PBs count for less. PBs imported from Strava or Apple show the times you ran; once your current training volume is known, a PB three years old carries half the weight of a fresh one inside the model, and a one-line note appears wherever that changes a number.

### Reads your recovery, not just your training
- Sleep quality, HRV, resting HR, and sleep debt all feed today's readiness score.
- Personal baselines, not population averages. Your "low HRV" is judged against your own 28-day norm.
- Leg fatigue tracked separately from cardiovascular load. Heavy eccentric days are flagged before they break you.
- Cumulative sleep debt is modelled with exponential decay, capped surplus credit, and a forward outlook.

### Built-in coaching
- AI Coach answers questions about your plan, your recovery, and what to do today. (Hidden for App Store v1 behind `AI_COACH_ENABLED`; see AI Coach v3 section.)
- A rules-based coach picks a stance every morning (Ready to Push, On Track, Manage Load, Ease Back) with a one-line explanation.
- Workout notes after every session: what went well, what was off, and what it means for the week.

### Numbers that update themselves
- FTP, CSS, VDOT, and threshold pace auto-update from your training when the evidence is strong enough.
- Manually entered values yield to better numbers, so the system never holds you to an old test.
- Per-station HYROX benchmarks and personal pace offsets calibrate from every race and every test.

### Real data, real safety
- Acute-to-chronic workload ratio enforced with continuous gradients, not arbitrary caps.
- Quality sessions are downgraded automatically when load climbs too fast.
- Triathlon adds per-discipline fitness, fatigue, and form so a hard bike week doesn't sabotage a run block.

### Works with what you already use
- Strava, Garmin, and Apple Watch sync automatically. Strava is treated as canonical when both report the same activity.
- **Manual "Sync" pulls full history** (Account → Apple Watch → Sync, 2026-06-22): the background syncs use a cheap 14-day incremental window, but tapping Sync forces a full-history backfill (`syncAppleHealth({ forceFullHistory: true })` clears `appleHistoryFetched` + `appleDeepHistoryFetched`). So a user whose rides/swims/runs never ingested can recover months of training — and re-derive bike/swim/run benchmarks from real activity — without resetting their plan. The non-destructive equivalent of a reset. `appleHealthSync.ts`, `account-view.ts btn-sync-apple`. ⚠️ native HealthKit path, not device-verified.
- Built-in GPS tracker for users who'd rather record in the app directly. Includes guided runs and live splits.
- Activity matching writes back to the plan, so a recorded session counts toward your week without manual logging.
- File upload (.fit, .gpx, .tcx) and full export (.zip) for users without live sync — your data, your portability right.
- Apple Health is the universal pipe: any watch that writes to it (Apple Watch directly; Garmin, COROS, Polar once the user enables Apple Health in that watch's own app) flows in without partner approval — our HealthKit query does not filter by source. Garmin via Apple Health arrives thin (avg HR + time, no route/zones), so any activity can be enriched by uploading its original file ("Add detail from Strava"), and a guided screen walks you through bringing your full Strava history. Discovery: users who are not Strava-connected and have ≥3 thin activities see a dismissible Home banner ("Add heart rate and splits to your runs") that drives the one-shot guided archive import, so the enrich path is found without digging into a single activity. The thin-activity test is the canonical `isThinActivity()` (`activity-enrich-merge.ts`), shared by the per-activity affordance and the banner. Single-file upload uses `ingestActivityFile()` (`fitImport.ts`): drop any one activity file and it auto-attaches to the matching thin run by timestamp (enrich in place, no duplicate) or imports as new — the user never has to find the run. A native "Share to Mosaic" seam (`window.__mosaicIngestSharedFile`, `shared-file-intake.ts`) lets an iOS share-sheet open call the same path; native wiring spec in `docs/SHARE_TO_MOSAIC.md` (pending rebuild). This matters only for non-Apple watch users outside the Strava beta — Apple Watch writes full data (route/HR stream) to Health, and Garmin bridges only average HR.
- Add a run from a screenshot: snap a summary from any run app, the on-device reader (iOS) or cloud reader (web) pulls out distance, time, splits, and HR.

## Engineering catalogue

The rest of this document is internal: every shipped feature with file paths, key functions, and test status.

## HYROX Mode (Phase 0 — foundation + wizard, behind `eventType === 'hyrox'`)

HYROX is a fully-separate mode for the hybrid run+functional-station racing format. Selected at onboarding (goals tile now active). Phase 0 delivers the complete onboarding wizard flow and state foundation; Phase 1 delivers the plan engine; Phase 2 delivers the full UI.

### H0. HYROX Foundation (Phase 0)
**What it ships**: Types (`AbilityBand`, `HyroxStation`, `HyroxConfig`, `HyroxSessionKind`), `EventType: 'hyrox'`, `Workout.musculoTendonLoad`, `SimulatorState.hyroxConfig`. Constants file (`hyrox-constants.ts`): MTL caps by band, time-to-band thresholds, station MTL factors (modality × impact), 3D load profiles, weekly session targets, plan duration table, eccentric-heavy station list. Mode detection helper (`isHyroxMode()`). MTL calculator (`computeComponentMTL`, `computeEffectiveMTL`).

**Onboarding wizard flow**: goals → connect-data → hyrox-setup → review → initializing → main-view. `hyrox-setup.ts` collects: competition format (Open or Pro, Singles or Doubles), division (doubles always; singles only when biological sex is unknown; lane H1, below), previous HYROX finish time (shows live ability band), race event (required — picked from `HYROX_WORLD_SERIES`; venue is derived from event id), weekly hours, sled/SkiErg/RowErg access. Picking an event sets `hyroxRaceEventId` + `customRaceDate` + `hyroxVenueId` together; the manual-date fallback shows a separate venue picker. **Continue gate (2026-10-05)**: once a previous time is typed, the format it was set in (Open or Pro, Singles or Doubles) is required; the reason sits next to Continue, a tap on the blocked button scrolls to the format pills, and the amber prompt updates live. Times under 8 run legs × the 3:10/km HYROX run floor (`HYROX_FINISH_FLOOR_SEC`, 25:20) ask for h:mm:ss, so "1:12" is no longer stored as a 72-second finish; a field that stops parsing clears the stored time. Tests ✅ `hyrox-setup-continue-gate.test.ts` (13). ⚠️ Not device-verified.

**Divisions and load tiers (2026-10-12/13, lane H1, `8352d0e`, SCIENCE_LOG §DG)**: the athlete races one of three rulebook load tiers (26/27 Singles and Doubles rulebooks): W-Open (sled push 102 kg, pull 78 kg including the sled, farmers 2 × 16 kg, sandbag 10 kg, ball 4 kg), M-Open (152 / 103, 2 × 24, 20, 6; also Women Pro, Doubles Men, Doubles Women Pro and both partners of a Mixed pair) and M-Pro (202 / 153, 2 × 32, 30, 9; Men Pro and Doubles Men Pro). Wall balls are 100 reps in every division, to 2.70 m for women and 3.00 m for men. `hyroxLoadTier(format, biologicalSex, division)` (`src/calculations/hyrox-division.ts:108`) is the one mapping; singles reads biological sex first, doubles reads the division pick first, and with neither the tier is `HYROX_DEFAULT_LOAD_TIER` (M-Open, ISSUE-395). Setup shows a Division row (`hx-toggle-row`) for doubles (Women / Men / Mixed; Mixed hidden under Pro Doubles, a Mixed pick cleared on that switch) and for singles only when biological sex is unset or "prefer not to say", prefilled from biological sex; stored as `OnboardingState.hyroxDivision` and `HyroxConfig.division` (both optional). The Pro note lists the athlete's own division loads ("Women Pro loads: sled push 152 kg, sled pull 103 kg, farmers carry 2 × 24 kg, sandbag 20 kg, wall ball 6 kg to 2.70 m."); before, every Pro athlete read the Men Pro loads. The doubles note reads "You run all 8 legs together and split the work inside every station. Training covers all 8 stations." The step's hours range, sessions default and plan-week default use the planning band (`setupBand`, `hyrox-setup.ts:56`, `hyroxPlanningBandFor`; lane H3c, SCIENCE_LOG §DL); known: the hours slider is not rescoped when the previous-race format is picked after the time (ISSUE-395 (k)). Tests ✅ `hyrox-setup-division.test.ts` (12), `hyrox-division.test.ts` (33), `constants/hyrox-rule-copy.test.ts` (3: no doubles 10-second rule, no current 75-rep rule, no single Men Pro load line). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Ability band derivation**: previous HYROX time maps to band through the planning band (below): the finish in Open Singles terms, banded on the Open Singles field of the athlete's own sex (lane H3c; the onboarding initializer since lane H2, `d8432d2`). The global thresholds (sub-1h=competitive, 1–1:20=advanced, 1:20–1:40=intermediate, 1:40–2h=novice, 2h+=beginner) no longer band anyone. Falls back to experience level slider when no time given. **Doubles previous times band on singles terms (2026-10-12/13, lane H1, SCIENCE_LOG §DH)**: the thresholds and seed times are singles times, so a doubles previous time converts with `doublesTotalInSinglesTermsSec` (the open_singles total at the same percentile of the ingested results) for either target, and a singles time stays as is (`hyroxBandingTimeSec`, `hyrox-doubles.ts:200`), in onboarding, Log a previous time, the previous-format fix and the setup step. The coaching-consensus × 1.22 and the unsourced × 0.85 are gone; a doubles-median previous time with a doubles target now bands intermediate (was advanced). *Superseded: lanes H3b and H3c replaced this rule (next paragraphs), and lane H2 (`d8432d2`) deleted `hyroxBandingTimeSec` and `hyrox-format-band.test.ts`.* Tests ✅ `hyrox-doubles.test.ts`, `state/hyrox-doubles-onboarding.test.ts` (16). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Field-relative bands (2026-10-13, lane H3b, `ee19331`, SCIENCE_LOG §DK)**: setup, log-a-race and the previous-format fix banded a previous time on the thresholds of the field it was raced in (`hyroxBandForPreviousTime`, `hyrox-results.ts:534`: each global threshold kept at its percentile in the S4 to S6 open singles field and re-expressed per format and sex; Open Singles women 68:55, 86:28, 110:17, 136:24). Lane H3c replaced that with one planning field for every format (§DL), and `hyroxBandForPreviousTime` has no app caller at `d8432d2`. The onboarding initializer banded on the global scale through `hyroxBandingTimeSec` until lane H2 (`d8432d2`) moved it to the same planning band. Tests ✅ `hyrox-results.test.ts`. ❌ Not device-verified.

**One planning band for every format (2026-10-13, lane H3c, `d7bc44b`, SCIENCE_LOG §DL)**: the ability band behind every planning input (the MTL cap, the setup step's hours range and sessions default, the default plan length when no race date is set, and the band seeds behind untested-station targets and the forecast) is read in one field: Open Singles at the athlete's own sex (biological sex, then a single-sex division pick, then the pooled Open Singles table). A Pro or Doubles previous finish converts to Open Singles terms first (`hyroxOpenSinglesTermsSec`, `src/calculations/hyrox-planning-band.ts:101`: the race field's split pattern at the finish's percentile, each part mapped to one athlete, Pro loaded stations converted to Open loads), then takes that field's thresholds (`hyroxPlanningBandFor`, `:141`). Open Singles women: competitive under 1:08:55, advanced to 1:26:28, intermediate to 1:50:17, novice to 2:16:24, beginner above; men 1:01:41, 1:18:28, 1:41:55, 2:07:07. Setup (`setupBand`, `hyrox-setup.ts:56`; before the previous format is picked the time reads as an Open Singles finish), Log a previous time, the previous-format fix and, since lane H2 (`d8432d2`), the onboarding initializer use it. Choosing Pro weights or a partner no longer lowers the band: a Pro Singles woman who raced 1:30:00 is 1:26:08 in Open Singles terms and plans advanced, where lane H3b's field-relative rule gave intermediate (docs-pass probe; at `d8432d2` the real initializer also gives advanced). Display ranks (Stats rank line, Where you sit, the review's Field position, station percentiles) stay in the athlete's own cohort. Known: stored bands are not re-derived when plans regenerate for generator version 18 (ISSUE-398 (a)). Tests ✅ `hyrox-planning-band.test.ts` (13), `hyrox-results.test.ts` (32), `state/hyrox-doubles-onboarding.test.ts` (16). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Initializer** (`initialization.hyrox.ts`): derives band → MTL cap → session counts → plan duration → calls `generateHyroxPlan(s)` which populates `wk.triWorkouts` on every week. **Resolved fitness first (2026-10-12, `a711d08`, SCIENCE_LOG §DB)**: the launch refresh (`refreshBlendedFitness`) runs before the staleness re-band and the personal run-pace offset, which read the store default `s.v` 50 before (10K 38:00 with a 58:00 race 800 days ago: re-banded competitive to advanced at VDOT 55.2, was intermediate; the offset is frozen once set), and again at the end on the committed plan, so onboarding equals the first launch. Test ✅ `src/state/hyrox-init-resolved-fitness.test.ts`. ❌ Not device-verified.

**MTL model** (`mtl.ts`): `rawMTL = IL × modalityFactor × impactFactor × (1 + externalFactor)` where `IL = durationMin × sRPE`. Saturation curve: `effectiveMTL = cap × (1 - exp(-raw/cap))`. Station and run intensity factors from research doc §3.3.

**Key files**: `src/types/triathlon.ts` (types), `src/types/state.ts` (musculoTendonLoad, hyroxComponents), `src/types/onboarding.ts` (wizard step + HYROX fields), `src/constants/hyrox-constants.ts`, `src/constants/hyrox-benchmarks.ts` (population seed times), `src/calculations/hyrox-mode.ts`, `src/calculations/mtl.ts`, `src/ui/wizard/steps/hyrox-setup.ts`, `src/state/initialization.hyrox.ts`

### H1. HYROX Plan Engine (Phase 1)
**What it ships**: Full Week[] plan generation with real workouts (no more skeleton weeks).

**Population benchmarks** (`hyrox-benchmarks.ts`): band seed times from the real HYROX results since 2026-10-13 (lane H3b, SCIENCE_LOG §DK). For each band of a format x sex field, the seed is the median split of the finishers whose total falls in that band (`HYROX_S89_BAND_SEEDS`, seasons 8 and 9); run pace is the band's median run total ÷ 8 and Roxzone its median; total_beginner takes the beginner cell. `stationSeedTimesFor`, `seedRunPaceSecKmFor` and `seedRoxzoneSecFor` (`hyrox-benchmarks.ts:62`, `:73`, `:82`) read one cell; the exported `STATION_SEED_TIMES_SEC`, `SEED_RUN_PACE_SEC_KM` and `SEED_ROXZONE_SEC` are Open Singles with women and men pooled. Callers that know the athlete read `hyroxStationSeedsAtTarget` (the athlete's field at the target's loads). Until lane H3b the seeds were hand-set from HyroxDataLab, RoxLyfe and Hyroxy.com aggregates, Open division, sexes averaged.

**Real HYROX results tables, seasons 7 to 9 (2026-10-13, lane H3a, `3e4b08d`, SCIENCE_LOG §DJ)**: `src/data/hyrox-results-s79.ts`, generated by `scripts/ingest-hyrox-results-s79.py` from the Kaggle dataset "HYROX Race Results, Seasons 7-9 (Anonymised)" (public HYROX results via pyrox-client; aggregate tables only, no rows). 1,142,946 of 1,159,421 results pass the hygiene rules. Percentile tables split by format (Open and Pro, Singles and Doubles) and sex (women, men, mixed pairs) cover the total, run total, work total, Roxzone and each of the 8 stations, on breakpoint grids sized by the DKW rule; cells under 119 finishers are withheld (Pro Doubles mixed, 39 rows). Also: total-time tables by age group (5-year bands for singles, 10-year for doubles), a seasons 8 and 9 set for the recent field (wall balls and sled push step up from season 8), per-season medians, same-venue season shifts, venue spread, median run-leg splits and repeat-athlete improvement (median first-to-latest change −4.1%; about 4% in every ability band once grouped by the mean of both races). Data only at `3e4b08d`: no screen reads it; lane H3b wired it later. Tests ✅ `src/data/hyrox-results-s79.test.ts` (29 at `3e4b08d`). No user-facing surface to device-verify at `3e4b08d`.

**Plan engine** (`plan_engine.hyrox.ts`): Phase-based periodisation (base=0–40%, build=40–75%, peak=75–90%, taper=90–100% of plan). Volume ramping via phase multiplier (same pattern as triathlon engine: 0.55–0.85 base, 0.92 build, 1.0 peak, stepped taper). Deload every 4th week (×0.70). Weekly hours from `hyroxConfig.weeklyHoursAvailable` × multiplier, split 40/35/25 across runs/stations/bricks. Session mix progresses by phase: base=all easy+technique; build=adds tempo+density; peak=intervals+density dominant. MTL cap enforced by proportional scaling if weekly MTL exceeds band cap.

**Execution → next-week load (2026-06-01, P2 / SCIENCE_LOG §AU)**: HYROX is no longer purely phase-driven. `hyroxEffortMultiplier` (`effort-multiplier.hyrox.ts`) reads trailing RPE-vs-expected across **all** HYROX sessions (stations + bricks + runs) over the last 2 completed weeks and scales the weekly budget: `effectiveHours = hours × phaseMult × deload × effortMult`. Same formula/bounds as running and tri (`1 − score × 0.05`, [0.85, 1.15]), same under-duration guard, applied to weeks ≥ current only, non-compounding. RPE-primary (the objective station-adherence signal doesn't exist for synced activities and is a deferred guided-only enhancement; HR rejected for intermittent station work). Volume/density is the correct HYROX lever because race implements are fixed-weight. Surfaced inline on the plan-view current-week summary when the shift is ≥5% ("Load raised/eased N% this week after recent sessions were rated easier/harder than planned").

**Generators** (`hyrox-generators.ts`): 7 session kinds → `Workout` with `hyroxComponents[]`. **Round count fills the time budget** for all circuit sessions (SCIENCE_LOG §AO): rounds = `floor(budget ÷ roundDuration)` clamped to the band's coach range (beginner 2–4, novice/intermediate 4–6, advanced/competitive 6–8, from research §3.5), and the headline `estimatedDurationMin` is computed from the prescribed work+rest, not the raw slot. Station technique: up to 4 stations at 65% seed pace, 60s/3min rest, + easy run finish. Station density: 2 race-discriminator stations + 1 erg at race pace, 30s/2min rest (research §7.2 "station work for time"). Full brick: N rounds × (1km run + 1 key station). Mini brick: beginner variant (500m + erg). Rounds + rests are stored on the workout (`hyroxRounds`, `hyroxRoundRestSec`, `HyroxComponent.restAfterSec`) so the detail modal and guided player read one source. All sessions compute `musculoTendonLoad` via `computeComponentMTL` (now scaling with actual rounds).

**Week 1 Station Calibration Test** (`generateHyroxAssessment` in `hyrox-generators.ts:481`): Every HYROX user gets the assessment session in Week 1, replacing the first station slot (`plan_engine.hyrox.ts:255-260`). One easy pass through all accessible stations at RPE 4, low MTL by design. Times entered after the session calibrate `stationBenchmarks` for plan personalisation. Users with prior race-derived benchmarks still receive it as an optional re-test — they skip/replace it like any other workout if they don't want it.

**Scheduler** (`scheduler.hyrox.ts`): Constraint-based day assignment (not rigid template). Priority order: bricks first → intervals → density → tempo → technique → easy runs. Bricks → Saturday; high-intensity sessions spaced ≥2 days apart.

**Key files**: `src/workouts/plan_engine.hyrox.ts`, `src/workouts/hyrox-generators.ts`, `src/workouts/scheduler.hyrox.ts`, `src/constants/hyrox-benchmarks.ts`, `src/calculations/effort-multiplier.hyrox.ts` (execution→load multiplier)

**Slot ids (2026-10-06, generator v14)**: `generateWeekSessions` stamps every session `W{week}-{type}-{idx}` (`stampTriWorkoutIds`, the triathlon engine's helper), and Add session gives added sessions `adhoc-` ids, so two same-named sessions ("Easy Run" twice a week) tick, sync and price independently. The generator-version regen lives in `src/state/hyrox-plan-regen.ts` (`applyHyroxGeneratorRegen`, called from `main.ts`): it carries `adhoc-` and `holiday-` sessions, gives legacy added sessions an id instead of deleting them, re-keys name-keyed ratings, records, matches and GPS recordings to the new ids (`rekeyNameKeyedHyroxSlots`), and sends an activity no single same-named session can claim to Activity Review. Commute runs carry `estimatedDurationMin` at the generator easy pace (`hyroxEasyRunPaceSecKm`), so ticking one records load. With ids in place the execution loop above is live (next-week volume 0.85× to 1.15× by session RPE, completed weeks only since `fed8bfc`); the ratings it reads include HR-derived ones from the matcher (ISSUE-340). Tests ✅ `hyrox-slot-ids.test.ts`, `hyrox-commute-duration.test.ts`, `effort-multiplier.hyrox-slot-record.test.ts`, `effort-multiplier-completed-weeks.test.ts`.

**Race calendar (2026-10-07/08, generator v16, SCIENCE_LOG §CW)**: 1- and 2-week plans split taper first ([taper], [peak, taper]). Plan length is the calendar weeks from the local Monday through the week holding race day (`racePlanCalendarWeeks`, `hyrox-setup.ts` and `initializeHyroxSimulator`), and the generator places race week on running's layout (`storedPlanRaceLayout`): the week holding the day 3 days before race day, with an empty closing week (`Week.raceClosing`) for a Monday to Wednesday race. For a Thursday to Saturday race the week before race week keeps nothing in the 6 days before race day; its full brick moves to 7 or more days out, never next to another hard session (`clearStoredPlanRaceWindow`, `scheduler.hyrox.ts` `isHighIntensity`). Nothing is generated on or after race day. `HYROX_GENERATOR_VERSION` was 16 for this change: the load-accounting branch's v14 (slot ids) and the race-calendar branch's v14 and v15 were bumped independently and merged in `c5f4a7f`; it is 17 since the race-week taper (next paragraph; `plan_engine.hyrox.ts:72`). The launch regen (`applyHyroxGeneratorRegen`) carries the closing-week flag. Forecast: the horizon ends with race week (`raceForecastHorizonISO`), `plannedHyroxSessionsPerWeek` skips the closing week (and since 2026-10-21 starts at the current week, `wks[s.w - 1]`; it read from next week), and race-day readiness (recovery signals) still applies in the closing week; since v17 a Monday to Wednesday race's race week holds the same sessions as the Sunday race before it and forecasts identically, `projectedTotalSec` included (until v17 it could read up to 3 s faster because its race week kept a Sunday easy run at R−1). Race week itself is placed relative to race day since v17 (next paragraph). Tests ✅ `src/state/race-plan-race-week.test.ts`, `src/state/race-closing-week-forecast.test.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-volume.test.ts`, `src/workouts/race-window.stored-plan.test.ts` (HYROX spacing), `src/workouts/race-day-drop.tri-hyrox.test.ts`. ❌ Not device-verified.

**Race week (2026-10-08, generator v17, SCIENCE_LOG §CZ)**: race week, the plan's final training week, is a taper placed relative to race day. No brick or Mini Brick (the race is the week's brick). The tempo run is the one hard session and the primer, on running's primer day (`raceWeekPrimerDow` from its Tuesday home day `HYROX_TEMPO_HOME_DOW`, `scheduler.hyrox.ts:40`): Tuesday when that is 3 to 5 days out, otherwise the latest race-week day in that window, so a Monday to Wednesday race has it 3 days out. Technique circuits use the ergs and the farmer carry only (`RACE_WEEK_EXCLUDED_STATIONS`, `hyrox-constants.ts:356`: no sleds, burpee broad jumps, lunges or wall balls) and sit at least 3 days out; without SkiErg and RowErg fewer than `MIN_CIRCUIT_STATIONS` (2) stations remain and the circuit slots are left out, so race week holds only runs. Easy runs take the latest free days, so the last 2 days hold easy runs only. No assessment. One session a day, nothing on or after race day; `raceWeekDays` (`plan_engine.hyrox.ts:243`) gives each session its allowed days and `scheduleHyroxWeek(sessions, band, allowedDays)` leaves out a session with no free allowed day (race-week commutes included, ISSUE-381). The content rules also apply to the final week of a plan without a race date. Example, Saturday race at 8 h: Mon circuit, Tue tempo, Wed circuit, Thu easy, Fri easy (132 min against a 144-min budget). Known: low-hours race weeks can exceed the 0.30 budget because of the session floors, and the Add session modal reschedules race week without these rules (ISSUE-383). Tests ✅ `src/state/hyrox-race-week.test.ts` (scenario hyrox, no ergs, Wednesday race, no race date, every race weekday × band × format × hours × station access, commutes), `src/workouts/hyrox-race-week-units.test.ts` (excluded stations, `scheduleHyroxWeek` with allowed days). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Load tiers and station targets (2026-10-12/13, lane H1, SCIENCE_LOG §DG, §DH)**: every station, brick and assessment generator computes station MTL from the athlete's tier (`stationExternalLoadKg(hyroxLoadTierForState(s))`: sleds at 0.5 × the tier's sled weight, farmers, sandbag and ball at the tier's load), including the gym session generator's HYROX brick in any mode and the mini brick's farmer-carry fallback; the old fixed table mixed tiers. Station target times read the forecast's per-station benchmark, one athlete's time over the full station at the target's weights, to the whole second (`hyroxPlanningStationBenchmarks`, `race-prediction.hyrox.ts:474`; plan engine `plan_engine.hyrox.ts:352` and the add-session modal): a singles athlete whose times came from doubles race splits trains to their converted times instead of seeds, a doubles athlete is no longer prescribed the pair's race split as a solo target (about 30% too fast), and an Open athlete with a Pro race split trains to its Open equivalent. Wall balls read "100 reps". `HYROX_GENERATOR_VERSION` was not bumped (still 17), so stored plans keep their old MTL and targets until a regeneration (lane H2). Tests ✅ `workouts/hyrox-load-tier-callers.test.ts` (5), `workouts/hyrox-planning-benchmarks.test.ts` (11). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Plan targets for untested stations (2026-10-13, lane H3b, `f4b7bf7`, `0ae4c96`, SCIENCE_LOG §DK)**: `hyroxPlanningStationBenchmarks` (`race-prediction.hyrox.ts:769`) returns all 8 stations: the calibrated benchmark, else the band seed at the target's weights (`hyroxStationSeedsAtTarget`), the number the Stats ledger and the forecast show. A Pro or women's target no longer trains untested stations to the generators' Open, sexes-pooled seed, and a woman in Mixed Doubles trains to Women Pro tier targets at her M-Open loads (ISSUE-395 (c) note). The guided-session best check reads `hyroxCalibratedStationBenchmarks` (`:753`, calibrated stations only), so a seed is never a benchmark to beat. At this merge the generators' own fallbacks (run pace, the week-1 assessment) read the pooled seed tables, now real Open Singles band medians (a pinned week-2 plan moved from 220 to 223 planned minutes), and `HYROX_GENERATOR_VERSION` stayed 17. Lane H2 (`d8432d2`) passes the athlete to every generator so those fallbacks read the same seeds, and bumps the version to 18, so stored plans regenerate on the next launch (`applyHyroxGeneratorRegen`). Tests ✅ `workouts/hyrox-planning-benchmarks.test.ts` (13), `hyrox-mixed-woman-loads.test.ts` (12). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Test status**: ⚠️ `hyrox-generators.test.ts` (7 tests of `generateHyroxStation`: station density and station technique round counts, budget fill, headline duration and rest encoding) and `effort-multiplier.hyrox.test.ts` (11) cover the station generators and the effort multiplier. The scheduler (`scheduler.hyrox.ts`) has unit tests since 2026-10-08 (`hyrox-race-week-units.test.ts`: race-week allowed days, and every session placed without them); the week generator (`generateWeekSessions`) has tests for slot ids, commute duration and race week (above).

### H3. HYROX MTL Load Model (Phase 3)

**MTL fitness-fatigue** (`src/calculations/mtl.ts`): `computeWeekMTL(wk)` sums planned `musculoTendonLoad` across `wk.triWorkouts`. `computeMTLFitnessFatigue(weeks, currentWeekIndex)` applies Banister EMA (CTL τ=42d / ATL τ=7d). Returns `{ mtlCTL, mtlATL }` daily-equivalent. `HyroxConfig` stores `mtlCTL?` and `mtlATL?`.

**Launch refresh** (`src/main.ts`): `if (state.eventType === 'hyrox')` block recomputes MTL CTL/ATL on every boot. Stores on state, saves, logs `[hyrox] MTL CTL/ATL refreshed: CTL=X ATL=Y ACWR=Z`.

**MTL ACWR readiness floor** (`src/calculations/readiness.ts`): New `mtlAcwr?: number | null` on `ReadinessInput`. Hard floors: MTL ACWR >1.5 → readiness ≤34, >1.3 → ≤54. New `'mtlLoad'` on `hardFloor`/`DrivingSignal` unions. Transferable to any mode with eccentric data. Home view passes `mtlAcwr` for HYROX users.

**Taper eccentric warning** (`src/ui/hyrox/workout-card.ts`): Within 10 days of race, cards with burpee BBJ / sandbag lunges / wall balls show amber inline note. Informational only — no plan mutation.

**MusculoTendon Load drill-down** (`src/ui/mtl-load-view.ts`): Detail page mirroring leg-fatigue's structure. Hero ring shows this week's actual MTL (derived from completed workouts at render time) vs cap, coloured by ACWR zone. 12-week chronic + acute PMC chart reads `computeMTLFitnessFatigue(weeks, i)` — which iterates **completed** weekly MTL via `computeWeekActualMTL` (a workout contributes when `isMtlSessionCompleted` passes: a match link or a record at the slot key, `mtl.ts:107`; mirrors how running CTL/ATL is fed by actual TSS). Cards: ACWR with zone verdict, discipline split (run / station / brick chronic share via `computeMTLFitnessFatigueByDiscipline`, also actuals-based), this-week contributors list (planned vs completed), explainer card. Two entry points: the MusculoTendon Load factor card on Readiness (`rdn-card-mtl`, click-through wired) and the MTL card on Hyrox Stats (`hx-stats-mtl-card`). All four numbers (actual, planned, chronic, acute) share one source convention.

**Key files**: `src/calculations/mtl.ts`, `src/calculations/readiness.ts`, `src/main.ts` (HYROX block), `src/ui/home-view.ts` (mtlAcwr wiring), `src/ui/hyrox/workout-card.ts` (taper warning), `src/ui/mtl-load-view.ts` (drill-down), `src/ui/readiness-view.ts` (factor card click-through), `src/ui/hyrox/stats-view.ts` (clickable MTL card)

**Completion and load ratio (2026-10-06)**: actual MTL counts a session as completed when it has a match link or an activity record on its slot key (`isMtlSessionCompleted`, `mtl.ts:107`): a synced match, GPS run, Mark as Done or station quick done. Before, only `matchedActivityId` counted, which HYROX never sets, so actual MTL was always 0 and every week advance cut the next week toward deload volume. Both EMAs start at the athlete's mean completed-week MTL (pre-plan weeks are missing, not rest), and the load ratio is withheld until 14 days of plan weeks are complete or while chronic is below 15 (`computeMtlAcwr` / `hyroxMtlAcwr`, `MTL_ACWR_MIN_CHRONIC`). That one computation feeds the readiness MTL floor (Home, Readiness and the daily coach's label and sentence), the Stats Load ratio row and tiles, and the MTL detail page, which now shows the current week. The Readiness MTL card shows the week's completed MTL like Stats. The forecast "Training fitness" factor and staleness Signal 2 compare chronic MTL with what the plan prescribed over the same completed weeks (`computeMtlChronicVsPlan`), shown from week 3. Ticked HYROX cross sessions (bike, row erg, swim) count as completed load but add 0 MTL (ISSUE-330). Open: build-entry weeks can trip the 1.3 floor for a plan-compliant athlete, and the Forecast chip detail still reads the launch snapshot `hx.mtlCTL` / `hx.mtlATL` (ISSUE-340). Tests ✅ `hyrox-mtl-completion.test.ts`, `hyrox-mtl-acwr-cold-start.test.ts`, `hyrox-mtl-vs-plan.test.ts`, `mtl-load-view.test.ts`, `readiness-mtl-card.test.ts`, `daily-coach-hyrox-mtl-floor.test.ts`.

**Test status**: ⚠️ Calculations covered (`mtl-by-discipline.test.ts` — 11 tests: planned and actual weekly MTL, discipline split, EMA fitness-fatigue from actuals, currentWeekIndex limit, fresh-user zero-state). Drill-down and Readiness MTL card covered: `mtl-load-view.test.ts` renders `renderMtlLoadView` (current week, a slot-record completion marked done, agreement with Stats and readiness) and `readiness-mtl-card.test.ts` renders `renderReadinessView` (a week of completed runs shows the same MTL as Stats). Not device-verified.

### H2. HYROX UI (Phase 2)

**Plan view** (`src/ui/hyrox/plan-view.ts`): Full week-by-week plan layout. Scroll atmosphere background + sun glint (same visual system as triathlon plan). Race countdown pill (top-right). Weekly summary strip: hours / sessions / MTL load. Three discipline mini-bars: Run (green) / Station (amber) / Brick (slate-blue). Week navigation pills (scrollable). Draft banner on future weeks. Benchmark calibration card surfaces on current week. Day-by-day layout using `renderHyroxWorkoutCard` with interactive tap + touch DnD reorder (iOS drag arms after a 350ms long-press so scrolling doesn't reorder days; "Hold and drag to reorder" hint above Monday on the current week). Each card carries a status chip top-right (Logged/Done green, Missed orange, Today terracotta, Adjusted/Skipped/Replaced/Holiday/Added) via the shared `src/ui/workout-status.ts` helper, matching the running plan card. 4-tab navigation (Home / Plan / Forecast / Stats). Since 2026-10-08 the header's "Week N of M" names the week being viewed, as the running Plan does (it read the current week while the phase label read the viewed week; test ✅ `ui/plan-header-viewed-week.test.ts`), and race day shows the display-only race-day card (feature 8.3).

**Workout card** (`src/ui/hyrox/workout-card.ts`): Three discipline flavours (run/station/brick) with colour-coded badges. Station/brick cards show `hyroxComponents` list (station name + distance/reps). MTL chip alongside RPE. "Tap for details" CTA (detail modal in Phase 3). Card class `hyrox-workout-card` + `data-hx-workout-id` / `data-hx-day-of-week` for DnD.

**Benchmark calibration card** (`src/ui/hyrox/benchmark-card.ts`): Mirrors tri `benchmark-tests-card.ts`. Shows up to 4 uncalibrated stations on current week. "Enter time" modal with mm:ss input; system doubles half-distance time → `hyroxConfig.stationBenchmarks[station]`. Dismissible via ×. Disappears once all accessible stations are calibrated. Forecast view's "Sharpen this forecast" CTA banner deep-links here and un-dismisses the card so a previously-hidden card resurfaces. **One calibrated count (2026-10-12/13, lane H1)**: the card counts through `hyroxCalibrationCoverage` (`hyrox-station-history.ts:262`), the forecast's per-station resolver: stations with an athlete time from either format's slot, out of all 8 raced stations, the same count the Forecast, the Stats Benchmarks card, the Account HYROX card and the chip detail "You" page show. It prompts only the pending stations the athlete has equipment for and names the rest ("Sled Push and Sled Pull need equipment not in your setup."). Before, Stats and Account read 0/8 beside a Forecast reading 8/8 for a singles target built on doubles splits, and the chip detail counted a station once per place it was stored. Tests ✅ `ui/hyrox/calibration-count-agreement.test.ts` (12), `ui/hyrox/benchmark-card-slot.test.ts` (4).

**Forecast-view calibration CTA** (`src/ui/hyrox/forecast-view.ts`): The standalone "Sharpen this forecast" banner was folded into the hero's **Stations** factor chip (count `calibrated/total` + "Test stations" CTA on the chip detail page) when the forecast adopted the marathon-style chip hero. The benchmark calibration card still lives on the plan view; its un-dismiss path is now reached via the Stations chip detail's CTA and the performance radar's "Calibrate" prompt, both of which navigate to the plan view and un-dismiss the card.

**Station benchmark history** (`src/calculations/hyrox-station-history.ts`, state field `hyroxConfig.stationBenchmarkHistory`): Append-only `{dateISO, sec, source, format, proWeights?}` per station. Mirrors `ftpHistory` / `cssHistory` from triathlon mode. Source enum: `'half_test' | 'full_test' | 'race' | 'manual'`. Helpers expose latest entry, age in months, best time per format, PR detection (latest beats all priors), and improvement vs previous entry. Backfilled on launch from existing scalar benchmarks with `'manual'` source and today's date. Drives the per-station table's inline percentile chip, "New PR" badge, and "Tested N mo ago" caption (forecast-view.ts:99-128). **Race splits recorded (2026-10-12, lane H1)**: onboarding and Log a previous time write one entry per split (source `'race'`, the race's format and `proWeights`, dated race day or today), and re-logging the same race (same date and format) replaces them (`upsertRaceSplitEntry`). The predictor reads the entry whose time matches each slot value (`benchmarkEntryForSlotValue`) to know whether a doubles value is the pair's time and which weights it was set at. Since lane H3b (2026-10-13, §DK) the history views read every entry in one frame through the forecast's own conversion (`hyroxStationHistoryInTargetFrame`, `race-prediction.hyrox.ts:524`), labelled once per card (ISSUE-395 (d) note). Tests ✅ `hyrox-station-history.test.ts`, `state/hyrox-doubles-onboarding.test.ts`.

**Per-station detail sub-page** (`src/ui/hyrox/station-detail-view.ts`): Tap any row in the per-station forecast table to drill down. Hero with current adjusted time + percentile, sparkline of historical entries (oldest → newest, faster = top), stats card (best, latest, population estimate, improvement since first test, race-day fade %), full history list with "Best" badge, and "Test again" CTA that un-dismisses the benchmark card and navigates to plan view. Since lane H3b (2026-10-13, §DK) every entry of every format renders in one frame, one athlete at the race's weights (`hyroxStationHistoryInTargetFrame`), each history row tagged by source and format ("Race split · Open Doubles"), with one caption on the first card in that frame when the target is doubles or an entry is converted ("Times show one athlete at your race's weights. Doubles race splits and times at other weights are converted with the forecast's ratios."). **Lane H1 (2026-10-12/13)**: a "Race standard" row gives the station's rulebook standard at the athlete's tier, labelled with the division (`hyroxStationRaceStandard`: damper 6 on both ergs, sleds as 4 × 12.5 m lanes with the sled weight, farmers, sandbag and ball at the tier's load, wall balls to the thrower's target); the percentile is a tier on the cohort's race splits ("· top 27%", lane H3b; was "est. pN" in lane H1 and "pN in your band" before); on a doubles page the hero reads "Pair's time from your benchmark" and the Stats row "Solo population estimate"; the estimate is the band seed at the target's weights (`hyroxStationSeedsForState`); "Race-day fade" shows only when the newest entry is a test or session best, never on a doubles page; times never print "m:60". Tests ✅ `ui/hyrox/results-wiring.test.ts` renders the page (tier chip, one frame label, no label for a singles target with nothing converted).

**"Where to gain" card** (`src/calculations/hyrox-weakest-link.ts` + `renderWhereToGainCard`, `forecast-view.ts:697`): glass card directly below "Where you lost time" (since 2026-10-20; the tab order is Where you lost time, Where to gain, Performance radar, Where you sit, Biggest targets). Ranks stations by absolute `gainableSec` (current adjusted time minus projected race-day time from the per-class horizon model) and shows the top 2 with their current → projected delta, reusing `prediction.projection.stations[].improvementPct` with no new constants. Since 2026-10-20 (lane rp, `55c0e20`) it adds the last race's biggest loss from "Where you lost time" when that is not in the top 2, captioned "Most time lost in your last race": a station by its horizon line (`weakestLinkRowFor`, `hyrox-weakest-link.ts:65`); Running by the Race order card's own average pace now → race day (`buildHyroxProjection` byDiscipline.run), with the run total's gain and "· 8-run total" in the caption; the Roxzone (or Running and Roxzone) by that projection's totals; "No gain at this dose" when nothing moves. The subhead names it (", plus the Roxzone, where your last race lost the most"). Gains read by the Forecast tab's one number rule (m:ss from a minute, "Ns" under). Hidden when no race date or no station has ≥ 1 sec gain. Tests ✅ `hyrox-weakest-link.test.ts` (9), `ui/hyrox/race-plan-lose-time-render.test.ts`.

**Goal modal** (`src/calculations/hyrox-goal-back-calc.ts` + `src/ui/hyrox/goal-modal.ts`; rebuilt 2026-10-20 as the race plan's goal step, SCIENCE_LOG §DO): opened by the Forecast hero's "Set a goal ›" and the race plan's "Change goal ›". Title "Set a goal", or "Change goal" with "Clear goal" when a goal exists. Pre-fills the forecast itself. Shows one feasibility sentence, the race plan's own (`hyroxGoalFeasibilityLine`, `hyrox-race-plan.ts:161`): "Inside your forecast's likely range.", "Faster than your forecast's likely range, 1:23:28 to 1:36:02.", "1:00:00 is out of reach even with every leg at top-1% level for Open Singles women.", "Matches your forecast." or "Your forecast already beats this goal." No targets list (the race plan carries the per-leg splits). The back-calculation (headroom-weighted, cap and redistribute, ISSUE-196) now floors each component at the athlete's own field's fast end (min of the top-1% median and p1, per format and sex or doubles division, venue seconds added), scales the Roxzone with the goal from the real results, and measures the gain from the forecast lines, so rounding never flags an achievable goal; a reachable goal faster than the hero's "Likely" range is a stretch (`hyroxLikelyRangeSec`). With no goal on file and the input at the forecast the button saves nothing and reads "Keep forecast" (singles, and on the race plan; closes) or "Open race plan" (doubles on the Forecast tab); it reads "Save goal" once the input differs. A saved goal opens the race plan. Persists to `hyroxConfig.targetFinishTimeSec`. **Parse disambiguation** (`parseHhMmSs`): a 2-part value reads as `mm:ss` unless that is below the legacy pooled competitive floor and the `h:mm` reading is plausible (floor to 4 h), so `1:00` resolves to 1 hour and `75:00` stays 75 min. The echo under the input ("Goal: H:MM:SS") shows only when the resolved value reads differently from the input. Tests ✅ `hyrox-goal-back-calc.test.ts` (18), `ui/hyrox/goal-modal.test.ts` (8), `ui/hyrox/race-plan-lose-time-render.test.ts` (button labels, keep and save flows). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Race plan** (`src/ui/hyrox/race-plan-view.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-race-plan.ts`; 2026-10-20, lane rp, `63c5942`, SCIENCE_LOG §DO): a drill-down from the Forecast tab with per-leg split targets and a race clock for the athlete's goal.
- *Entry*: the hero line "Goal 1:29:35 · Race plan ›"; with no goal, "Set a goal ›" opens the goal modal and a saved goal lands on the race plan. Doubles with no goal also get a "Race plan" row, "Race clock for every leg at your forecast." (the doubles tab has no Race order card). Singles never see a race plan without a goal.
- *Page*: the 16 legs in race order plus the Roxzone. With a goal: Goal, Clock (race clock at the end of the leg) and Forecast (muted, predictHyroxRace's own lines). With no goal or an unrealistic one: Split and Clock at the forecast. Subhead "Splits for your 1:29:35 goal. Forecast 1:34:35.", "Splits for your 1:34:35 goal. Matches your forecast." or "Splits for your 1:34:35 forecast." One feasibility line for a stretch or unrealistic goal (unrealistic adds "Splits below are at your forecast."). Caption: "Clock includes Roxzone time around each station. Run legs slow slightly as the race goes on.", plus the partners line for doubles. Fits 375 px.
- *Goal splits*: faster goal, the goal back-calculation on the field's fast-end floors with the Roxzone scaled to the goal; slower goal, each forecast component eased by how finishers at the goal time race it; run legs follow the forecast's own per-leg fade; whole seconds add up to the goal and the clock after Wall Balls is the goal. Doubles: everything in the pair's terms from the division's tables.
- *One clock*: the Race order card reads the same forecast clock (`hyroxForecastRaceClock`), headers Split / Clock, legs Run 1 to Run 8.
- Tests ✅ `hyrox-race-plan.test.ts` (32), `hyrox-peer-splits.test.ts` (14), `ui/hyrox/race-plan-lose-time-render.test.ts` (39, jsdom). ❌ Not device-verified.

**Where you lost time** (`src/ui/hyrox/lose-time-view.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-lose-time.ts`; 2026-10-20, lane rp, `63c5942`, SCIENCE_LOG §DO): the previous result, segment by segment, against athletes (pairs for doubles) who finished in the same time, in the field it was raced in (format × sex, or the doubles division recorded with the result).
- *Card* (directly above Where to gain): the headline ("Most time lost on Wall Balls: 1:12 slower than athletes finishing in 1:24:31.", "in the Roxzone" for the Roxzone; "Most time gained ..."; "Level with athletes finishing in 1:24:31 on every segment."), the next two material losses and "All segments ›".
- *Drill-down*: Time lost and Time gained rows ("41:20 against 38:24", ", from your finish" when implied), "Level with your peers: ..." in race order, a provenance caption naming the cohort, its finisher count and that venues differ, "Update result ›" and "Where training can gain ›".
- *Segments*: the 8 stations; Running as one segment (no leg named); the Roxzone, pasted or implied from the finish; with stations only, "Running and Roxzone". Level within 15 s plus the segment's own share of the peers' additivity gap.
- *Empty states*: "Raced HYROX before? Paste a result to compare each segment with athletes who finished in the same time." or "Add the splits from your 1:24:31 ...", which open Log a previous HYROX with "Race splits" first and expanded; "pairs" for a doubles result; no card for a total_beginner with no result.
- *Log a previous HYROX* (`log-prev-time-modal.ts`, `applyHyroxPrevTimeUpdate`): the paste also reads Run 1 to 8, the run total and the Roxzone (`parseHyroxResultsText`); the modal opens with the stored splits filled in; the same race is the same finish, event and date, and a format-only correction keeps its splits; a different race clears them; a doubles result records its division (required when no sex is on file); all four split fields are replaced together and only typed or pasted stations become benchmarks. Onboarding stores station splits only (ISSUE-402 (g)).
- *Forecast tab*: card titles in bold sentence case; one number rule for any time lost, gained or saved (m:ss from a minute, "Ns" under) across Where you lost time, Where to gain and Biggest targets; Biggest targets reads "6:50 now → 4:03 at competitive" with a neutral saving cell and sits last.

**Home view** (`src/ui/home-view.ts`): `buildTodayWorkoutHyrox()` reads today's `wk.triWorkouts` by day. `buildHyroxSessionBlock()` renders each Run/Station/Brick/Strength/Cross-train session as its own glass card (shared `buildHeroSessionCard` — `backdrop-filter` + faint ring + light title, same material as the running hero) with a colour-coded discipline badge and an `RPE · MTL` footer; separate cards with clear air between them, the "DAY · Today" eyebrow on the top card only. Race forecast card suppressed in HYROX mode. `getHomePlanName()` returns `'HYROX {Band}'`. Each card keeps the `hx-hero-card` class + `data-hx-hero-workout-id` so taps open the workout detail modal.

**Forecast view** (`src/ui/hyrox/forecast-view.ts`): Full race-time prediction (fully built). **Marathon-style glass hero** — centred "HYROX forecast" title with "{Name}'s {City} HYROX — Week X of Y" sub-line, then a single glass card carrying: predicted finish time (54 px light weight), a **likely range** line beneath (`Likely H:MM:SS–H:MM:SS`, the factor-based confidence band from `hyroxForecastBandPct` centred on the finish, read through `hyroxLikelyRangeSec`, which goal feasibility also reads — base ±4%, wider with weeks-out and low calibration; the deliberate alternative to running/tri's Monte Carlo cone), race-day delta beneath ("On pace vs goal" / "+X min vs goal" when a goal is set, worded "target" until 2026-10-20; otherwise "Projected −X min by race day" when projection has ≥60 s gain), a **calibration banner** on any non-high confidence: `low` shows a strong "Provisional forecast" panel + "Test your stations": below 3 calibrated stations its body says untested stations use the median split of the ability band in the HYROX results, and at 3 or more (a race-age cap) it gives the calibrated count (lane H3b); `medium` (partially calibrated, fully calibrated without a venue, or held there by converted stations) shows a soft "Sharpen your forecast" panel with the `X of N stations calibrated` count + "Calibrate the rest". When every station is converted from the other format the button reads "Test your stations" (`calibrationBannerCtaLabel`, lane H3c). Since 2026-10-13 (lane H1) a forecast that converts the other format's benchmarks says so in either tier (`crossFormatBannerBody`: "7 of 8 station times are converted from your doubles race splits.", the seeds still standing in, and "Converted times cap confidence at medium." only when that cap is what holds it at medium); cross-format no longer forces low. Both state that a station assessment makes the forecast more accurate and name the protocol ("each station at half distance, race effort"); CTA routes to the Stations factor page, a "Set a goal ›" line ("Goal H:MM:SS · Race plan ›" when set, which opens the race plan; both since 2026-10-20, see Race plan below), five HYROX-native factor chips (**You** / **Run pace** / **Stations** / **Training** / **Venue**), each tappable to a dedicated sub-page via `renderHyroxChipDetail(key)` (mirrors marathon mode via the parallel `src/ui/hyrox/forecast-chip-detail-view.ts`). Each sub-page leads with the headline number, follows with breakdown (band thresholds + prev race / VDOT + pace source + **Critical pace** row with a "View critical pace →" link to the dedicated CP detail page — ISSUE-274, CP read from the same `deriveHyroxRunPace` call so the two surfaces agree by construction; the old marathon Daniels-zone card was removed / per-station list with status / phase + sessions bar chart + MTL / per-factor course-factor breakdown), and closes with a science caption. Stations and Venue pages carry their own CTAs (Test stations → plan view; Change format → forecast modal), a "Missing: X, Y. Fewer factors mean lower confidence" caption when any chip is absent, and a previous-race + format-change link under the chips when prior time exists. Confidence badge top-right of the card toggles a detail panel with calibration / staleness / cross-format / VDOT-source / venue-equivalent notes. Legacy "Sharpen this forecast" CTA banner, "Race-day projection" card, and "Course factors" card all removed — their signals fold into the Calibration chip, the hero delta line, and the Venue chip detail respectively. **Race Order card** (singles): bordered-pill segment control with two views, each doing one thing. **Race order** = passive pacing clock: 16 rows in actual race sequence (R1 → SkiErg → R2 → … → R8 → Wall Balls), each row shows the current split plus the race **Clock** that builds top-to-bottom (headers Split / Clock and legs Run 1 to Run 8 since 2026-10-20; the one forecast clock, `hyroxForecastRaceClock`, which the race plan reads too). No projection arrows, no slip delta column, no click-through on station rows (drill-down lives on the other tab). Run rows keep the in-race fatigue bar (green/amber/red by slip %) as a passive visual signal. **Station order** = where you'll gain: all 8 stations grouped first, then all 8 runs grouped, every row (station OR run) shows `now → race day` with race-day gain delta sourced from `HyroxProjectionMarkers.stations[]` (per-station horizon model) for stations and the run-discipline ratio for legs. Unified column meaning across the whole view — no in-race fatigue bar on runs, no +slip delta. No cumulative total (ordering is by discipline). Station rows here remain tappable for the per-station detail sub-page; preserve the PR badge and "Tested N mo ago" caption when stale (≥4 mo). The clock adds the Roxzone half before and half after each station (since 2026-10-20; venue factors are already in the lines, and race-order station rows show the race-day split at the venue), so the clock after Wall Balls is the headline finish. No percentile chips — comparison would be apples-to-oranges (benchmarks are max-effort fresh, population data is mid-race fatigued). Footer: "X.X sessions/wk for N.N weeks. Gain rates anchored on rowing, LME, and HIFT adaptation literature; **run rows are driven by the running VDOT horizon** (`applyTrainingHorizonAdjustment` at `target_distance: '10k'`, run sessions/wk + bricks at 0.5, taper handled by HYROX factor model, see SCIENCE_LOG §AH). Taper is gradiented (Mujika 2002): gains shrink toward race day." **Doubles format** keeps the split layout (separate Run legs and Your stations cards); since 2026-10-12 (lane H1, SCIENCE_LOG §DH) Your stations lists all 8 stations as the pair's time, with a caption (`doublesStationsCaption`, `forecast-view.ts:372`) saying which stations map from the solo time station by station "at your level in the Open Doubles mixed results" (the pair's division, lane H3b) and which are doubles race splits used as is. With all 8 stations calibrated and none converted, no calibration banner shows (lane H3b). Before, it showed 4 "active" stations at singles time. Empty state when no time or benchmarks set. Venue is prescribed from the race event picked in onboarding. **Injury-aware projection** (2026-05-29, SCIENCE_LOG §AQ): an active injury debits each discipline's projection build weeks, stolen verbatim from the triathlon model. Run uses the `run` capacity; each station maps to a body region (`HYROX_STATION_REGION`: SkiErg=upper, Sled Push & Sandbag Lunges=lower, the rest full-body) and is debited by that region's `upperLoad`/`lowerLoad` recovery window (full-body stations take the worse of the two). A blocked region → no projected gain on its stations. Today's "race now" number is unchanged; only the race-day gain shrinks. The hero shows a one-line recovery note (`renderHyroxInjuryNote`, mirroring the tri marquee). When no explicit return-to-sport window was entered, the debit falls back to the injury protocol's typical-recovery midpoint (`injuryWithResumeFallback`, the same band the modal pre-fills), so the forecast responds to any restricted discipline. **Plan propagation**: the HYROX plan is gated only at generation time, so the injury modal save regenerates the current + future weeks (`regenerateHyroxWeek` → `applyHyroxInjuryGates`): blocked run drops runs, blocked upper/lower drops the mixed station circuits (`min(upper,lower)`), reduced scales durations. This also lowers the projection's planned-sessions input, so the forecast responds through both the per-discipline debit and reduced planned volume.

The Training drill-down's current week is `wks[s.w - 1]`: its phase, week label, weeks to go, completion chart ring and Sessions ahead read next week before. **Run-pace model v2 — Critical Pace anchor + Bayesian personalisation** (added 2026-05-12, see SCIENCE_LOG §AA): the previous run-pace derivation (`threshold × tier-ratio ≥ 1.05`) was structurally wrong for trained HYROXers — it implicitly assumed every athlete runs slower than threshold, when in fact trained athletes pace 1km HYROX legs at or faster than threshold because stations act as metabolic recovery. The v2 model anchors on **Critical Pace** (Hill 1923; Jones et al. 2010; Galbraith 2014), uses **continuous interpolation by VDOT** (no more discrete band steps), allows ratios < 1.0 for advanced/competitive tiers, and adds **format-specific pace conversion** for singles ↔ doubles (1.05–1.10 factor; *replaced 2026-10-12 by the data run ratio, `doublesRunPaceFactor`, about 1.02 at P50: the pair runs at the slower partner's pace, SCIENCE_LOG §DH*). Each logged HYROX race feeds a **Bayesian personal offset** that biases future predictions toward the athlete's actual observed pace, decaying linearly to 0 over 12 months without fresh evidence. **Critical Pace card** on Stats view surfaces the full derivation chain (VDOT → threshold → CP → format → personal offset → HYROX pace). Tapping it opens a **drill-down detail page** (not a modal) that leads with **critical pace over time** — a real curve re-derived from VDOT history via the same `gp(vdot, lt).t × cpRatioForVdot(vdot)` formula the card uses, so the latest point equals the headline — then the derivation breakdown, then an explanation of what critical pace is, why it matters, and the scientific rationale. Wiring: `src/calculations/hyrox-run-pace.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-personal-pace.ts`, `src/state/initialization.hyrox.ts` (initial offset from onboarding race data), `src/ui/hyrox/stats-view.ts`, `src/ui/hyrox/critical-pace-detail-view.ts`, `buildCpTrendChart` in `src/ui/triathlon/benchmark-charts.ts`.

**Run paces displayed to the nearest second (2026-10-12, `ced8427`)**: the critical-pace detail, forecast, forecast-chip and run labels format through `fpModel` / `fmtModelPaceKm`, so a threshold of 299.6 s/km reads 5:00/km (it read 4:60/km). The run pace itself is still rounded inside the model (ISSUE-390). The run-pace note carries the PB-age caption when PB ageing moves the derived run pace by at least 1 s/km (§DC).

**Doubles forecast, benchmark resolution and honest labels (2026-10-12/13, lane H1, `8352d0e`, SCIENCE_LOG §DH, §DI)**:
- *Doubles forecast from the results data* (`src/calculations/hyrox-doubles.ts`): the pair's station times are singles times × the open_doubles ÷ open_singles station ratio at the athlete's percentile (about 0.71 at P50), Roxzone × its own ratio (about 0.94, no longer halved), and the run pace × the run ratio (about 1.02: the pair runs at the slower partner's pace). The old model (4 whole stations per partner, halved Roxzone and fatigue rate, doubles runs 5 to 9% faster) put an intermediate pair at 58:04, faster than 98%; a median singles athlete now forecasts within 3% of the doubles median (1:19:07), labelled 40 to 60%. Pro Doubles is placed on the Pro scale in singles terms. *Since lane H3b (§DK) the ratios are per station and per division on the S8 to S9 tables, and Pro Doubles reads its own rows.*
- *Cross-format benchmarks, per station*: a singles target reads a doubles race split ÷ the station ratio and a solo test in the doubles slot as is; a doubles target reads singles values × the ratio. Each station comes from the target's slot when it holds that station, else the other slot (`resolveHyroxBenchmarks`). A converted station counts as calibrated and caps confidence at medium in either direction; the copy names the cap only when it binds (`crossFormatCapped`).
- *One number per station*: the Stats Benchmarks rows (`hyroxBenchmarkLedger`, captions "Converted from doubles race", "Solo equivalent of doubles race", "From singles benchmark"), plan and add-session station targets and the guided-session best check read the forecast's own value (`resolveHyroxStationBenchmarks`): one athlete's time at the target's weights, so a slower round never replaces a faster converted split and a Pro split is read at Open weights for an Open target. Session bests are stored with the weights they were done at.
- *Honest labels*: station percentiles read "est. pN" (they are placed on the band seed times; the results data has no per-station splits), the radar subtitle says so, the targets card carries `STATION_PERCENTILE_CAVEAT`, and the guided summary reads "Estimated pN on the band seed times". `hyroxPopulationCaption` (distributions card, Stats rank card, onboarding review) names the format's finisher count, women and men together, seasons 4 to 6 (2021/22 to 2023/24), the 75-rep Women Open era and, for Open Doubles, the pooled pairs. The forecast hero detail and the methodology page say the station times are the athlete's benchmarks or band seed times and the results data sets only the finish percentiles and doubles ratios (no "calibrated against 89,868 finishers"). *Replaced 2026-10-13 by lane H3b (§DK): station percentiles are real race-split percentiles shown as tiers, and every caption reads `hyroxResultsCaption` (S8 to S9, by sex or division).*
- *Forecast optimism*: the projection's "Weekly volume" factor is removed (a double count), adherence counts planned slots with a record, and stations the plan never programmes bank no gain.
- *Times*: HYROX times round before the minute split (`ftRounded`), never "m:60".

### H4. HYROX Activity Matching (Phase 4)

**Station matching** (`src/calculations/activity-matcher.ts`): In HYROX mode, station-type activities (`HIIT`, `STRENGTH_TRAINING`, `CROSS_TRAINING`, `FUNCTIONAL_TRAINING`, `CARDIO`, `AEROBIC_TRAINING`, `INDOOR_CARDIO`) bypass the cross-training path and are matched against planned station/brick `triWorkouts`. Matching criteria: same day of week (±1 day tolerance) and duration within 50–200% of planned. Auto-complete on match: writes `wk.rated`, `wk.garminActuals`, accumulates `hyroxConfig.weeklyMTL` from the matched workout's `musculoTendonLoad`. No match: past weeks → adhoc, current week → review queue.

**HYROX run matching**: Running activities in HYROX mode match against planned run-discipline `triWorkouts` (bypasses the running engine's `regenerateWeekWorkouts`). Match criteria: day (±1) + distance ±25% or duration ±30%. Matched actuals carry `hrEffortScore` and `paceAdherence` identical to running mode.

Both paths write `garminPending` entries so ActivityReview can surface them for re-rating.

**Key files**: `src/calculations/activity-matcher.ts` (lines ~890–1065 — HYROX guard blocks)

**Since 2026-10-06**: Activity Review matches HYROX activities against `wk.triWorkouts` (it used a running week the HYROX plan never shows), and `matchAndAutoComplete` builds no running week in HYROX, so a run the HYROX branches miss waits for review instead of completing a phantom Threshold. Synced Tempo and Interval runs auto-match on duration (`parseDistanceKm` read "10 min" as 10 miles). Apple HIIT arrives as 'HIIT' and can complete a station session. A GPS run started from a HYROX run card completes that card and its watch copy replaces the record in place. A station quick done and run Mark as Done carry load (§20c), and an in-app station weighs as strength in Signal A. Cross-training overflow is logged with no running modal, and the Home "Reduce session load" button is hidden (a HYROX version is an open decision, ISSUE-332). Tests ✅ `hyrox-no-phantom-running-slots.test.ts`, `walk-never-takes-run-slot.review.test.ts`, `parse-distance-minutes.test.ts`, `recording-handler.multisport.test.ts`, `plan-changes-running-only.test.ts`.

**Test status**: ⚠️ The 2026-10-06 changes above are covered by the listed tests (they include HYROX station and run matcher paths); the station duration and day-tolerance rules have no dedicated test. Real-sync check still manual.

### H5. HYROX Doubles partner (2026-10-13, lane HP)

**What it does** (merged in `d8bdff7`, SCIENCE_LOG §DM; tables live in production since 2026-10-13, RUNBOOK "HYROX Doubles partner tables"): two Mosaic users racing HYROX Doubles link by an invite code. Each then sees the other's station benchmarks against their own, the pair's run pace and a pair forecast. Tristan's rules (2026-10-12): link by code, share only race numbers, either partner can unlink at any time, one partner at a time.

**Entry** (`src/ui/hyrox/stats-view.ts:289`): a "Partner" row at the bottom of Stats, in doubles formats only. Its value reads "Link" when unlinked and the partner's initials when linked, and stays blank until the link has been read this session, so a linked athlete never sees "Link" flash. A foreground re-read that finds a new or ended link updates the label in place.

**Not linked** (`src/ui/hyrox/partner-view.ts`, `renderHyroxPartnerView` `:141`): "Create code" shows the code as "ABCD-EFGH" with a Copy button (the clipboard, or the code text selected when the clipboard is refused) and "Single use. Expires in 24 h." One live code per user: a new code deletes the earlier one. "Partner's code" takes the code with or without the dash, in any case. While a code is on screen the page re-reads the link every 5 s; away from the page, the app re-reads it on foreground. Two partners entering each other's codes, at once or seconds apart, both end up linked. Each error is one line: "This code has expired or been used. Check the 8 characters." (unknown, expired and used codes share it), "Check the code. It has 8 characters.", "This is your own code. Your partner enters it on their phone.", "You already have a partner. Unlink first.", "That athlete already has a partner.", "Offline. Connect and try again.", "Something went wrong. Try again."

**Linked**: the partner's initials and "Updated 13 Oct"; one row per station ("You lead by 12 s", "Partner leads by 8 s", "Level", or "Not comparable" with a reason: "No benchmark from you", "Pair time from a doubles race", "Race setups differ", "Load not known", "Not at the race loads"); Run pace, the slower partner's solo pace ("Your partner's solo pace, the slower of the two"); Pair forecast, an equal split of every station before course factors. A withheld forecast reads "Not available" and names what is missing: "Needs matching race setups.", "Station loads not known for your partner." or "Needs times at Men Open loads from your partner." A Mixed pair compares every station, because a woman's Women Open tests reach her snapshot at Men Open loads through lane H3b's per-sex conversions. Unlink asks inside the page: "Unlink from AB? Neither of you will see the other's numbers."

**What the partner sees** (`src/calculations/hyrox-partner-snapshot.ts`, one typed row in `hyrox_partner_snapshots`): format, division, race tier, header initials, per-station benchmarks with their load tier and pair-time flags, the athlete's own forecast station lines and Roxzone, and their singles run pace. VDOT, LT, heart rate, sleep, activities, biological sex, the name and the plan never leave the device through this path.

**Retention**: ending a link (either partner, Wipe everything, account deletion) deletes both partners' snapshots and any code either holds, and no row then records who linked with whom. A snapshot can be written only while its owner is linked. Account > Edit Plan away from doubles deletes the athlete's own snapshot and keeps the link; Reset plan deletes it at once and keeps the link only if the new setup ends in doubles. A Wipe or Reset plan made offline is retried at the next launch, per account.

**Production**: migration applied 2026-10-13 through the SQL editor; the data-export and account-deletion edge-function changes are in source only. Until they are deployed, the export file has no partner tables. The app itself deletes partner data on account deletion and Wipe everything.

**Privacy disclosure** (`7fdf0d0`, 2026-10-19; ISSUE-399 (a), not marked fixed): the privacy policy (`public/privacy.html`, last updated 19 October 2026) no longer says nothing is shared with other users; a "HYROX Doubles partners" section lists what a link shares (station times, run pace, Roxzone time, forecast splits, race format, division and initials), says nothing else is shared, and that unlinking, a wipe or account deletion removes it. The unlinked Partner page shows one line before linking (`PARTNER_SHARING_DISCLOSURE`, `partner-view.ts:369`): "Linking shares your station times, run pace, Roxzone time, forecast splits, race format, division and initials with your partner. Nothing else. Either of you can unlink at any time." The landing site copy (`~/Documents/mosaic-landing`, `9c57ff4`, `privacy.html` and `support.html`) is committed and not deployed; it goes live with the release that includes partner matching (RUNBOOK, HYROX Doubles partner tables).

**Key files**: `src/ui/hyrox/partner-view.ts`, `src/ui/hyrox/stats-view.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-partner-snapshot.ts`, `src/calculations/hyrox-partner-compare.ts`, `src/data/hyroxPartner.ts`, `src/data/hyroxPartnerSync.ts`, `src/ui/events.ts` (reset hooks), `src/ui/account-legal.ts`, `supabase/migrations/20261013000001_hyrox_partner.sql`, `supabase/rls-tests/20261013000001_hyrox_partner_rls_test.sql`.

**Test status**: ✅ 136 unit tests at `5068d8b`: `hyrox-partner-snapshot.test.ts` (16), `hyrox-partner-compare.test.ts` (21), `data/hyroxPartner.test.ts` (31), `data/hyroxPartnerSync.test.ts` (33), `data/hyroxPartnerSync.chunk.test.ts` (1), `ui/hyrox/partner-view.test.ts` (34; 35 since `7fdf0d0`, the disclosure test). ⚠️ The RLS test script passed on throwaway local PostgreSQL in every review round and has not run against production. ❌ Not device-verified.
